def best_interval(work, intervals, ckpt_cost, restart_cost, mtbf, seeds):
    return intervals[0], 0.0
--- solution
import math
import random


def simulate_run(work, interval, ckpt_cost, restart_cost, failures):
    t, saved, progress, hit = 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0

    def next_failure():
        return failures[hit] if hit < len(failures) else math.inf

    def fail():
        nonlocal t, progress, hit
        t = next_failure() + restart_cost
        hit += 1
        while next_failure() < t:
            t = next_failure() + restart_cost
            hit += 1
        progress = 0.0

    while True:
        segment = min(interval - progress, work - saved - progress)
        if next_failure() < t + segment:
            fail()
            continue
        t += segment
        progress += segment
        if saved + progress >= work:
            return round(t, 6), hit
        if next_failure() < t + ckpt_cost:
            fail()
            continue
        t += ckpt_cost
        saved += progress
        progress = 0.0


def failure_times(mtbf, horizon, seed):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    t, out = 0.0, []
    while True:
        t += rng.expovariate(1 / mtbf)
        if t > horizon:
            return out
        out.append(t)


def best_interval(work, intervals, ckpt_cost, restart_cost, mtbf, seeds):
    runs = [failure_times(mtbf, 100 * work, s) for s in seeds]
    best = None
    for iv in intervals:
        mean = sum(simulate_run(work, iv, ckpt_cost, restart_cost, f)[0] for f in runs) / len(runs)
        if best is None or mean < best[1]:
            best = (iv, mean)
    return best[0], round(best[1], 3)
--- hint
Generate every seed's failure list once, before trying the intervals, and reuse the lists for every candidate.
--- hint
Keep the best (interval, mean) pair, replacing it only on a strictly smaller mean.
--- check case | Near Young's interval of 0.775 hours
best_interval(100, [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0], 0.1, 0.2, 3.0, range(40))
=> (0.75, 139.478)
--- check case | Cheaper checkpoints: a shorter best interval
best_interval(100, [0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0], 0.01, 0.2, 3.0, range(40))[0]
=> 0.25
--- check case | A rare failure rate makes long intervals fine
best_interval(50, [1.0, 5.0, 10.0, 25.0], 0.1, 0.2, 1000.0, range(10))[0]
=> 25.0
--- check case | One candidate
best_interval(20, [2.0], 0.1, 0.2, 3.0, range(5))[0]
=> 2.0

=== mlsys1-gate | ML Systems I: mastery gate
--- teach
The gate covers the whole course: the accelerator cost model and the roofline, number formats and loss scaling, the memory of a training step, data parallelism and the collectives, ZeRO, tensor and pipeline parallelism, planning a run, the input pipeline, and failures and checkpoints. Each problem starts from the course's simulator, so you build on code you know, but every problem is new and most combine two or more lessons. To get ready, redo the practice problems of the lessons that felt hardest, and make sure you can derive the bubble fraction, the ZeRO memory per stage, the ring all-reduce time and Young's interval without looking.
--- gate
pass 7
questions 8
minutes 120

+++ problem | Attention, fused and unfused, on the roofline
--- task
One attention head over a sequence of s tokens with head size d runs three kernels when it is not fused:

- scores: an s × d by d × s multiply, 2·s·s·d FLOPs, reading Q and K (s·d numbers each) and writing the s × s scores;
- softmax: 5 FLOPs per score, reading and writing the s × s scores;
- output: an s × s by s × d multiply, 2·s·s·d FLOPs, reading the s × s scores and V (s·d numbers) and writing the s × d output.

A **fused** kernel does all the same FLOPs but keeps the scores in on-chip memory: it reads Q, K and V and writes the output, 4·s·d numbers in all.

Write `attention_times(accel, seq, heads, head_dim, bytes_per)` returning `(unfused_us, fused_us)`: the lower-bound time of all `heads` heads, each kernel timed with `accel.time`, in microseconds rounded to 3 places. The starter has the course's `Accelerator` and `ACCEL`.
--- starter
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


def attention_times(accel, seq, heads, head_dim, bytes_per):
    return (0.0, 0.0)
--- solution
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


def attention_times(accel, seq, heads, head_dim, bytes_per):
    s, d, bp = seq, head_dim, bytes_per
    unfused = (
        accel.time(2 * s * s * d, bp * (2 * s * d + s * s))
        + accel.time(5 * s * s, bp * 2 * s * s)
        + accel.time(2 * s * s * d, bp * (s * s + 2 * s * d))
    )
    fused = accel.time(4 * s * s * d + 5 * s * s, bp * 4 * s * d)
    return round(heads * unfused * 1e6, 3), round(heads * fused * 1e6, 3)
--- check case | A 2,048-token sequence, 32 heads of 128
attention_times(ACCEL, 2048, 32, 128, 2)
=> (380.284, 69.391)
--- check case | Four times longer
attention_times(ACCEL, 8192, 32, 128, 2)
=> (5816.102, 1110.249)
--- check case | A short sequence
attention_times(ACCEL, 128, 8, 64, 2)
=> (0.524, 0.175)
--- check case | A toy accelerator with whole-number speeds
attention_times(Accelerator("toy", 100, 10, 1), 2, 1, 1, 1)
=> (2400000.0, 800000.0)

+++ problem | Promoting partial sums in a low-precision dot product
--- task
Some low-precision matrix units accumulate in the narrow format, so long dot products lose everything past the point where the sum stalls. The fix is to **promote**: every `block` products, add the narrow partial sum into an fp32 total and start a new partial sum at 0.

Write `blocked_dot(xs, ys, fmt, block)`. Round each input to `fmt`, multiply, and round the product to `fmt`; add it to the partial sum and round to `fmt`. After every `block` products, add the partial sum to the total with `round_to(total + partial, FP32)` and reset the partial sum. At the end, add the last partial sum the same way and return the total. If `block` is `None`, never promote: return the partial sum. The starter has `round_to` and the formats from lesson 02.
--- starter
import math

FP32 = (8, 23, True)
BF16 = (8, 7, True)
FP16 = (5, 10, True)
E4M3 = (4, 3, False)


def bias(fmt):
    return 2 ** (fmt[0] - 1) - 1


def largest(fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if has_inf:
        return (2 - 2.0 ** -man_bits) * 2.0 ** bias(fmt)
    return (2 - 2.0 ** (1 - man_bits)) * 2.0 ** (bias(fmt) + 1)


def round_to(x, fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if math.isnan(x) or x == 0:
        return x
    top = largest(fmt)
    if math.isinf(x):
        return x if has_inf else math.copysign(top, x)
    a = abs(x)
    power = max(math.frexp(a)[1] - 1, 1 - bias(fmt))
    spacing = 2.0 ** (power - man_bits)
    q = round(a / spacing) * spacing
    if q > top:
        q = math.inf if has_inf else top
    return math.copysign(q, x)


def blocked_dot(xs, ys, fmt, block):
    return sum(x * y for x, y in zip(xs, ys))
--- solution
import math

FP32 = (8, 23, True)
BF16 = (8, 7, True)
FP16 = (5, 10, True)
E4M3 = (4, 3, False)


def bias(fmt):
    return 2 ** (fmt[0] - 1) - 1


def largest(fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if has_inf:
        return (2 - 2.0 ** -man_bits) * 2.0 ** bias(fmt)
    return (2 - 2.0 ** (1 - man_bits)) * 2.0 ** (bias(fmt) + 1)


def round_to(x, fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if math.isnan(x) or x == 0:
        return x
    top = largest(fmt)
    if math.isinf(x):
        return x if has_inf else math.copysign(top, x)
    a = abs(x)
    power = max(math.frexp(a)[1] - 1, 1 - bias(fmt))
    spacing = 2.0 ** (power - man_bits)
    q = round(a / spacing) * spacing
    if q > top:
        q = math.inf if has_inf else top
    return math.copysign(q, x)


def blocked_dot(xs, ys, fmt, block):
    total, partial, count = 0.0, 0.0, 0
    for x, y in zip(xs, ys):
        product = round_to(round_to(x, fmt) * round_to(y, fmt), fmt)
        partial = round_to(partial + product, fmt)
        count += 1
        if block is not None and count == block:
            total = round_to(total + partial, FP32)
            partial, count = 0.0, 0
    if block is None:
        return partial
    return round_to(total + partial, FP32)
--- check case | 4,096 ones in bf16: stalls at 256 without promotion
(blocked_dot([1.0] * 4096, [1.0] * 4096, BF16, None), blocked_dot([1.0] * 4096, [1.0] * 4096, BF16, 128))
=> (256.0, 4096.0)
--- check case | A block too long still stalls inside each block
blocked_dot([1.0] * 4096, [1.0] * 4096, BF16, 300)
=> 3524.0
--- check case | Products that are not exact
(blocked_dot([0.1] * 1000, [0.3] * 1000, BF16, None), blocked_dot([0.1] * 1000, [0.3] * 1000, BF16, 32))
=> (8.0, 30.5146484375)
--- check case | fp8 e4m3
(blocked_dot([0.5] * 100, [0.5] * 100, E4M3, None), blocked_dot([0.5] * 100, [0.5] * 100, E4M3, 16))
=> (4.0, 25.0)
--- check case | Empty vectors
(blocked_dot([], [], BF16, 4), blocked_dot([], [], BF16, None))
=> (0.0, 0.0)

+++ problem | The longest sequence a plan can train
--- task
Write `max_seq_for_plan(accel, model, plan, global_seqs)`: the largest whole sequence length s ≥ 1 for which `plan_memory(model, plan, s, global_seqs)` (lesson 07's memory model, in the starter) is at most `accel.mem_bytes`, or 0 if not even s = 1 fits. Memory grows with s, partly with its square. Search: double an upper bound until it no longer fits, then binary search; the answers run to tens of thousands.
--- starter
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


class Model:
    """The shape of a GPT-style transformer (lesson 03)."""

    def __init__(self, name, layers, hidden, heads, vocab):
        self.name = name
        self.layers = layers
        self.hidden = hidden
        self.heads = heads
        self.vocab = vocab

    def params(self):
        return 12 * self.layers * self.hidden ** 2 + self.vocab * self.hidden


def state_bytes(model):
    return 16 * model.params()


def layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch):
    return seq * micro_batch * (34 * model.hidden + 5 * model.heads * seq)


def activation_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    if recompute == "none":
        return model.layers * layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return model.layers * 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return model.layers * 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden + layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


class Cluster:
    """Devices in nodes, joined by links (lesson 04). Ranks 0 .. size()-1, node by node."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, per_node, accel, intra_bw, intra_lat, inter_bw, inter_lat):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.per_node = per_node
        self.accel = accel
        self.intra_bw = intra_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, between two devices in one node
        self.intra_lat = intra_lat    # seconds of latency per message inside a node
        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes
        self.inter_lat = inter_lat    # seconds of latency per message between nodes
        self.log = []                 # every message sent: (src, dst, nbytes)

    def size(self):
        return self.nodes * self.per_node

    def node_of(self, rank):
        return rank // self.per_node

    def message_time(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        if self.node_of(src) == self.node_of(dst):
            return self.intra_lat + nbytes / self.intra_bw
        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw

    def send(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        """Send one message: record it and return how long it takes."""
        self.log.append((src, dst, nbytes))
        return self.message_time(src, dst, nbytes)


def make_cluster(nodes, per_node):
    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


MATMUL_EFFICIENCY = 0.7   # assumption: well-tuned matrix multiplies reach about 70% of peak


def ring_time(p, nbytes, alpha, bw):
    """Alpha-beta time of a ring all-reduce of nbytes over p ranks on identical links."""
    if p == 1:
        return 0.0
    return 2 * (p - 1) * (alpha + nbytes / (p * bw))


def per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    """Activation bytes one layer keeps per micro-batch (lesson 03), before tensor parallelism."""
    if recompute == "none":
        return layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


def candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
    """Every plan (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute) that uses the whole cluster legally."""
    devices = cluster.size()
    for tp in range(1, cluster.per_node + 1):
        if cluster.per_node % tp or model.heads % tp:
            continue
        for pp in range(1, model.layers + 1):
            if model.layers % pp or devices % (tp * pp):
                continue
            dp = devices // (tp * pp)
            if global_seqs % dp:
                continue
            per_rank = global_seqs // dp
            for micro_batch in range(1, per_rank + 1):
                if per_rank % micro_batch:
                    continue
                for recompute in ("none", "selective", "full"):
                    yield (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute)


def plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    slice_params = model.params() / (tp * pp)
    state = 4 * slice_params + 12 * slice_params / dp          # ZeRO stage 1 over dp
    in_flight = min(pp, m)                                     # 1F1B: stage 0 holds this many
    act = in_flight * (model.layers // pp) * per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute) / tp
    if recompute == "full":
        act += layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch) / tp       # the one layer being rebuilt
    return state + act


def plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    devices = dp * tp * pp
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    flops = (8 if recompute == "full" else 6) * model.params() * seq * micro_batch / (tp * pp)
    compute = flops / (cluster.accel.peak_flops * MATMUL_EFFICIENCY)
    act = seq * micro_batch * model.hidden * 2
    tp_comm = (model.layers // pp) * 4 * ring_time(tp, act, cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    if pp == 1:
        p2p = 0.0
    elif tp * pp <= cluster.per_node:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.intra_lat + act / cluster.intra_bw)
    else:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.inter_lat + act / cluster.inter_bw)
    per_micro = compute + tp_comm + p2p
    if devices > cluster.per_node:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.inter_lat, cluster.inter_bw)
    else:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    return (m + pp - 1) * per_micro + dp_comm


def mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    step = plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
    useful = 6 * model.params() * seq * global_seqs
    return useful / (step * dp * tp * pp * cluster.accel.peak_flops)


def best_plan(model, cluster, seq, global_seqs):
    best = None
    for plan in candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
        if plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs) > cluster.accel.mem_bytes:
            continue
        u = mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
        if best is None or u > best[1]:
            best = (plan, u)
    if best is None:
        return None
    return best[0], round(best[1], 4)


def max_seq_for_plan(accel, model, plan, global_seqs):
    return 0
--- solution
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


class Model:
    """The shape of a GPT-style transformer (lesson 03)."""

    def __init__(self, name, layers, hidden, heads, vocab):
        self.name = name
        self.layers = layers
        self.hidden = hidden
        self.heads = heads
        self.vocab = vocab

    def params(self):
        return 12 * self.layers * self.hidden ** 2 + self.vocab * self.hidden


def state_bytes(model):
    return 16 * model.params()


def layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch):
    return seq * micro_batch * (34 * model.hidden + 5 * model.heads * seq)


def activation_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    if recompute == "none":
        return model.layers * layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return model.layers * 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return model.layers * 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden + layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


class Cluster:
    """Devices in nodes, joined by links (lesson 04). Ranks 0 .. size()-1, node by node."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, per_node, accel, intra_bw, intra_lat, inter_bw, inter_lat):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.per_node = per_node
        self.accel = accel
        self.intra_bw = intra_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, between two devices in one node
        self.intra_lat = intra_lat    # seconds of latency per message inside a node
        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes
        self.inter_lat = inter_lat    # seconds of latency per message between nodes
        self.log = []                 # every message sent: (src, dst, nbytes)

    def size(self):
        return self.nodes * self.per_node

    def node_of(self, rank):
        return rank // self.per_node

    def message_time(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        if self.node_of(src) == self.node_of(dst):
            return self.intra_lat + nbytes / self.intra_bw
        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw

    def send(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        """Send one message: record it and return how long it takes."""
        self.log.append((src, dst, nbytes))
        return self.message_time(src, dst, nbytes)


def make_cluster(nodes, per_node):
    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


MATMUL_EFFICIENCY = 0.7   # assumption: well-tuned matrix multiplies reach about 70% of peak


def ring_time(p, nbytes, alpha, bw):
    """Alpha-beta time of a ring all-reduce of nbytes over p ranks on identical links."""
    if p == 1:
        return 0.0
    return 2 * (p - 1) * (alpha + nbytes / (p * bw))


def per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    """Activation bytes one layer keeps per micro-batch (lesson 03), before tensor parallelism."""
    if recompute == "none":
        return layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


def candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
    """Every plan (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute) that uses the whole cluster legally."""
    devices = cluster.size()
    for tp in range(1, cluster.per_node + 1):
        if cluster.per_node % tp or model.heads % tp:
            continue
        for pp in range(1, model.layers + 1):
            if model.layers % pp or devices % (tp * pp):
                continue
            dp = devices // (tp * pp)
            if global_seqs % dp:
                continue
            per_rank = global_seqs // dp
            for micro_batch in range(1, per_rank + 1):
                if per_rank % micro_batch:
                    continue
                for recompute in ("none", "selective", "full"):
                    yield (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute)


def plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    slice_params = model.params() / (tp * pp)
    state = 4 * slice_params + 12 * slice_params / dp          # ZeRO stage 1 over dp
    in_flight = min(pp, m)                                     # 1F1B: stage 0 holds this many
    act = in_flight * (model.layers // pp) * per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute) / tp
    if recompute == "full":
        act += layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch) / tp       # the one layer being rebuilt
    return state + act


def plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    devices = dp * tp * pp
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    flops = (8 if recompute == "full" else 6) * model.params() * seq * micro_batch / (tp * pp)
    compute = flops / (cluster.accel.peak_flops * MATMUL_EFFICIENCY)
    act = seq * micro_batch * model.hidden * 2
    tp_comm = (model.layers // pp) * 4 * ring_time(tp, act, cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    if pp == 1:
        p2p = 0.0
    elif tp * pp <= cluster.per_node:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.intra_lat + act / cluster.intra_bw)
    else:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.inter_lat + act / cluster.inter_bw)
    per_micro = compute + tp_comm + p2p
    if devices > cluster.per_node:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.inter_lat, cluster.inter_bw)
    else:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    return (m + pp - 1) * per_micro + dp_comm


def mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    step = plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
    useful = 6 * model.params() * seq * global_seqs
    return useful / (step * dp * tp * pp * cluster.accel.peak_flops)


def best_plan(model, cluster, seq, global_seqs):
    best = None
    for plan in candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
        if plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs) > cluster.accel.mem_bytes:
            continue
        u = mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
        if best is None or u > best[1]:
            best = (plan, u)
    if best is None:
        return None
    return best[0], round(best[1], 4)


def max_seq_for_plan(accel, model, plan, global_seqs):
    def fits(s):
        return plan_memory(model, plan, s, global_seqs) <= accel.mem_bytes

    if not fits(1):
        return 0
    lo, hi = 1, 2
    while fits(hi):
        lo, hi = hi, hi * 2
    while lo + 1 < hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if fits(mid):
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return lo
--- check case | The 13B model's best plan from lesson 07
max_seq_for_plan(ACCEL, Model("13B", 40, 5120, 40, 50304), (8, 1, 8, 1, "none"), 1024)
=> 2579
--- check case | Selective recomputation buys much longer sequences
max_seq_for_plan(ACCEL, Model("13B", 40, 5120, 40, 50304), (8, 1, 8, 1, "selective"), 1024)
=> 10221
--- check case | Tensor parallelism with full recomputation
max_seq_for_plan(ACCEL, Model("13B", 40, 5120, 40, 50304), (8, 8, 1, 1, "full"), 1024)
=> 51916
--- check case | A plan whose model state alone does not fit
max_seq_for_plan(ACCEL, Model("70B", 80, 8192, 64, 50304), (1, 1, 1, 1, "full"), 8)
=> 0
--- check case | A 7B model with four-way tensor parallelism
max_seq_for_plan(ACCEL, Model("7B", 32, 4096, 32, 50304), (2, 4, 1, 1, "none"), 64)
=> 6614

+++ problem | All-to-all over the cluster
--- task
In an **all-to-all**, every rank has a separate chunk of data for every other rank: `buffers[i][j]` is the list rank i must send to rank j. Afterwards, rank j holds `out[j][i] = buffers[i][j]` for every i (its own chunk `buffers[j][j]` stays put). Expert-parallel layers use it to send each token to the device that holds its expert.

Write `all_to_all(cluster, buffers, bytes_per)` with the **pairwise exchange** schedule: in step s = 1, 2, …, p − 1, every rank i sends its chunk for rank (i + s) mod p, with `cluster.send(i, (i + s) % p, len(chunk) * bytes_per)`. A step takes as long as its slowest message. Return `(out, time)`, with `out[j]` the list of the p chunks rank j ends up with, in order of source rank, as new lists. The starter has the course's `Cluster` and `make_cluster`.
--- starter
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


class Cluster:
    """Devices in nodes, joined by links (lesson 04). Ranks 0 .. size()-1, node by node."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, per_node, accel, intra_bw, intra_lat, inter_bw, inter_lat):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.per_node = per_node
        self.accel = accel
        self.intra_bw = intra_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, between two devices in one node
        self.intra_lat = intra_lat    # seconds of latency per message inside a node
        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes
        self.inter_lat = inter_lat    # seconds of latency per message between nodes
        self.log = []                 # every message sent: (src, dst, nbytes)

    def size(self):
        return self.nodes * self.per_node

    def node_of(self, rank):
        return rank // self.per_node

    def message_time(self, src, dst, nbytes):

            return self.intra_lat + nbytes / self.intra_bw
        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw

    def send(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        """Send one message: record it and return how long it takes."""
        self.log.append((src, dst, nbytes))
        return self.message_time(src, dst, nbytes)


def make_cluster(nodes, per_node):
    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


def all_to_all(cluster, buffers, bytes_per):
    return [list(b) for b in buffers], 0.0
--- solution
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)

class Cluster:
    """Devices in nodes, joined by links (lesson 04). Ranks 0 .. size()-1, node by node."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, per_node, accel, intra_bw, intra_lat, inter_bw, inter_lat):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.per_node = per_node
        self.accel = accel
        self.intra_bw = intra_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, between two devices in one node
        self.intra_lat = intra_lat    # seconds of latency per message inside a node
        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes
        self.inter_lat = inter_lat    # seconds of latency per message between nodes
        self.log = []                 # every message sent: (src, dst, nbytes)

    def size(self):
        return self.nodes * self.per_node

    def node_of(self, rank):
        return rank // self.per_node

    def message_time(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        if self.node_of(src) == self.node_of(dst):
            return self.intra_lat + nbytes / self.intra_bw
        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw

    def send(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        """Send one message: record it and return how long it takes."""
        self.log.append((src, dst, nbytes))
        return self.message_time(src, dst, nbytes)


def make_cluster(nodes, per_node):
    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


def all_to_all(cluster, buffers, bytes_per):
    p = len(buffers)
    out = [[None] * p for _ in range(p)]
    for i in range(p):
        out[i][i] = list(buffers[i][i])
    total = 0.0
    for s in range(1, p):
        slowest = 0.0
        for i in range(p):
            j = (i + s) % p
            slowest = max(slowest, cluster.send(i, j, len(buffers[i][j]) * bytes_per))
            out[j][i] = list(buffers[i][j])
        total += slowest
    return out, total
--- check case | Three ranks: the chunks are transposed
all_to_all(make_cluster(1, 3), [[[1], [2], [3]], [[4], [5], [6]], [[7], [8], [9]]], 4)[0]
=> [[[1], [4], [7]], [[2], [5], [8]], [[3], [6], [9]]]
--- check test | p(p - 1) messages, step s going s ranks ahead
(lambda c: (all_to_all(c, [[[0.0] * 10 for _ in range(4)] for _ in range(4)], 2), len(c.log) == 12 and [(s, d) for s, d, n in c.log][4:8] == [(0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 0), (3, 1)])[1])(make_cluster(2, 2))
--- check test | Every step on two nodes crosses the slow network
abs(all_to_all(make_cluster(2, 2), [[[float(i * 4 + j)] * 1000 for j in range(4)] for i in range(4)], 2)[1] - 3 * (10e-6 + 2000 / 50e9)) < 1e-15
--- check test | Inside one node the fast links set the time
abs(all_to_all(make_cluster(1, 4), [[[1.0] * 600 for j in range(4)] for i in range(4)], 4)[1] - 3 * (3e-6 + 2400 / 300e9)) < 1e-15
--- check test | One rank keeps its own chunk, with no time
all_to_all(make_cluster(1, 1), [[[1, 2]]], 4) == ([[[1, 2]]], 0.0)

+++ problem | A loss-scaled step that every replica agrees to skip
--- task
With fp16 gradients and data parallelism, one replica's gradients can overflow while the others' do not. All replicas must then skip the step together, or their weights drift apart, and the overflowed numbers must never enter the gradient all-reduce, which would turn every replica's gradients into `inf` or NaN.

Write `scaled_dp_step(cluster, rank_grads, scale, fmt)`. Each rank r multiplies its gradients by `scale` and rounds them to `fmt` with `round_to`. Each rank sets a flag, 1.0 if any of its scaled values is not finite and 0.0 otherwise, and the ranks all-reduce the flags first (`ring_allreduce` of one-number lists, `bytes_per = 4`). If the result is above 0, return `(False, None, scale / 2)`. Otherwise all-reduce the scaled gradients (`bytes_per = 2`), divide rank 0's averaged values by `scale`, and return `(True, gradients, scale)`. The starter has the toolkit and `round_to`.
--- starter
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)

        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes

        return rank // self.per_node

        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw
