    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


def chunk_bounds(n, p):
    """Split n numbers into p nearly equal chunks: a list of (start, end) pairs."""
    return [(c * n // p, (c + 1) * n // p) for c in range(p)]


def reduce_scatter(cluster, buffers, bytes_per, ranks=None):
    """Ring reduce-scatter: position i ends up owning the sum of chunk i."""
    p = len(buffers)
    ranks = list(range(p)) if ranks is None else ranks
    bounds = chunk_bounds(len(buffers[0]), p)
    data = [list(b) for b in buffers]
    total = 0.0
    for step in range(p - 1):
        sends = []
        for i in range(p):
            lo, hi = bounds[(i - step - 1) % p]
            sends.append((i, lo, data[i][lo:hi]))
        slowest = 0.0
        for i, lo, payload in sends:
            j = (i + 1) % p
            slowest = max(slowest, cluster.send(ranks[i], ranks[j], len(payload) * bytes_per))
            for x, v in enumerate(payload):
                data[j][lo + x] += v
        total += slowest
    return [data[i][bounds[i][0]:bounds[i][1]] for i in range(p)], total


def all_gather(cluster, shards, bytes_per, ranks=None):
    """Ring all-gather: every position ends up with all the shards, in order."""
    p = len(shards)
    ranks = list(range(p)) if ranks is None else ranks
    bounds = []
    start = 0
    for s in shards:
        bounds.append((start, start + len(s)))
        start += len(s)
    data = [[0.0] * start for _ in range(p)]
    for i in range(p):
        lo, hi = bounds[i]
        data[i][lo:hi] = shards[i]
    total = 0.0
    for step in range(p - 1):
        sends = []
        for i in range(p):
            lo, hi = bounds[(i - step) % p]
            sends.append((i, lo, hi, data[i][lo:hi]))
        slowest = 0.0
        for i, lo, hi, payload in sends:
            j = (i + 1) % p
            slowest = max(slowest, cluster.send(ranks[i], ranks[j], len(payload) * bytes_per))
            data[j][lo:hi] = payload
        total += slowest
    return data, total


def ring_allreduce(cluster, buffers, bytes_per, ranks=None):
    """Average the buffers across positions: reduce-scatter, divide, all-gather."""
    p = len(buffers)
    shards, t1 = reduce_scatter(cluster, buffers, bytes_per, ranks)
    shards = [[v / p for v in s] for s in shards]
    full, t2 = all_gather(cluster, shards, bytes_per, ranks)
    return full, t1 + t2
import math

FP16 = (5, 10, True)


def bias(fmt):
    return 2 ** (fmt[0] - 1) - 1


def largest(fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if has_inf:
        return (2 - 2.0 ** -man_bits) * 2.0 ** bias(fmt)
    return (2 - 2.0 ** (1 - man_bits)) * 2.0 ** (bias(fmt) + 1)


def round_to(x, fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if math.isnan(x) or x == 0:
        return x
    top = largest(fmt)
    if math.isinf(x):
        return x if has_inf else math.copysign(top, x)
    a = abs(x)
    power = max(math.frexp(a)[1] - 1, 1 - bias(fmt))
    spacing = 2.0 ** (power - man_bits)
    q = round(a / spacing) * spacing
    if q > top:
        q = math.inf if has_inf else top
    return math.copysign(q, x)


def scaled_dp_step(cluster, rank_grads, scale, fmt):
    avg, _ = ring_allreduce(cluster, rank_grads, 2)
    return True, avg[0], scale
--- solution
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


class Cluster:
    """Devices in nodes, joined by links (lesson 04). Ranks 0 .. size()-1, node by node."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, per_node, accel, intra_bw, intra_lat, inter_bw, inter_lat):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.per_node = per_node
        self.accel = accel
        self.intra_bw = intra_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, between two devices in one node
        self.intra_lat = intra_lat    # seconds of latency per message inside a node
        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes
        self.inter_lat = inter_lat    # seconds of latency per message between nodes
        self.log = []                 # every message sent: (src, dst, nbytes)

    def size(self):
        return self.nodes * self.per_node

    def node_of(self, rank):
        return rank // self.per_node

    def message_time(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        if self.node_of(src) == self.node_of(dst):
            return self.intra_lat + nbytes / self.intra_bw
        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw

    def send(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        """Send one message: record it and return how long it takes."""
        self.log.append((src, dst, nbytes))
        return self.message_time(src, dst, nbytes)


def make_cluster(nodes, per_node):
    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


def chunk_bounds(n, p):
    """Split n numbers into p nearly equal chunks: a list of (start, end) pairs."""
    return [(c * n // p, (c + 1) * n // p) for c in range(p)]


def reduce_scatter(cluster, buffers, bytes_per, ranks=None):
    """Ring reduce-scatter: position i ends up owning the sum of chunk i."""
    p = len(buffers)
    ranks = list(range(p)) if ranks is None else ranks
    bounds = chunk_bounds(len(buffers[0]), p)
    data = [list(b) for b in buffers]
    total = 0.0
    for step in range(p - 1):
        sends = []
        for i in range(p):
            lo, hi = bounds[(i - step - 1) % p]
            sends.append((i, lo, data[i][lo:hi]))
        slowest = 0.0
        for i, lo, payload in sends:
            j = (i + 1) % p
            slowest = max(slowest, cluster.send(ranks[i], ranks[j], len(payload) * bytes_per))
            for x, v in enumerate(payload):
                data[j][lo + x] += v
        total += slowest
    return [data[i][bounds[i][0]:bounds[i][1]] for i in range(p)], total


def all_gather(cluster, shards, bytes_per, ranks=None):
    """Ring all-gather: every position ends up with all the shards, in order."""
    p = len(shards)
    ranks = list(range(p)) if ranks is None else ranks
    bounds = []
    start = 0
    for s in shards:
        bounds.append((start, start + len(s)))
        start += len(s)
    data = [[0.0] * start for _ in range(p)]
    for i in range(p):
        lo, hi = bounds[i]
        data[i][lo:hi] = shards[i]
    total = 0.0
    for step in range(p - 1):
        sends = []
        for i in range(p):
            lo, hi = bounds[(i - step) % p]
            sends.append((i, lo, hi, data[i][lo:hi]))
        slowest = 0.0
        for i, lo, hi, payload in sends:
            j = (i + 1) % p
            slowest = max(slowest, cluster.send(ranks[i], ranks[j], len(payload) * bytes_per))
            data[j][lo:hi] = payload
        total += slowest
    return data, total


def ring_allreduce(cluster, buffers, bytes_per, ranks=None):
    """Average the buffers across positions: reduce-scatter, divide, all-gather."""
    p = len(buffers)
    shards, t1 = reduce_scatter(cluster, buffers, bytes_per, ranks)
    shards = [[v / p for v in s] for s in shards]
    full, t2 = all_gather(cluster, shards, bytes_per, ranks)
    return full, t1 + t2
import math

FP16 = (5, 10, True)


def bias(fmt):
    return 2 ** (fmt[0] - 1) - 1


def largest(fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if has_inf:
        return (2 - 2.0 ** -man_bits) * 2.0 ** bias(fmt)
    return (2 - 2.0 ** (1 - man_bits)) * 2.0 ** (bias(fmt) + 1)


def round_to(x, fmt):
    exp_bits, man_bits, has_inf = fmt
    if math.isnan(x) or x == 0:
        return x
    top = largest(fmt)
    if math.isinf(x):
        return x if has_inf else math.copysign(top, x)
    a = abs(x)
    power = max(math.frexp(a)[1] - 1, 1 - bias(fmt))
    spacing = 2.0 ** (power - man_bits)
    q = round(a / spacing) * spacing
    if q > top:
        q = math.inf if has_inf else top
    return math.copysign(q, x)


def scaled_dp_step(cluster, rank_grads, scale, fmt):
    scaled = [[round_to(g * scale, fmt) for g in grads] for grads in rank_grads]
    flags = [[0.0 if all(math.isfinite(v) for v in values) else 1.0] for values in scaled]
    agreed, _ = ring_allreduce(cluster, flags, 4)
    if agreed[0][0] > 0:
        return False, None, scale / 2
    averaged, _ = ring_allreduce(cluster, scaled, 2)
    return True, [v / scale for v in averaged[0]], scale
--- check test | A good step: the average, unscaled
(lambda r: r[0] is True and r[2] == 1024.0 and abs(r[1][0] - 4e-6) < 1e-8 and abs(r[1][1] - 5e-6) < 1e-8)(scaled_dp_step(make_cluster(1, 4), [[1e-6, 2e-6], [3e-6, 4e-6], [5e-6, 6e-6], [7e-6, 8e-6]], 1024.0, FP16))
--- check case | One replica overflows: everyone skips and halves the scale
scaled_dp_step(make_cluster(1, 4), [[1e-6, 2e-6], [3e-6, 4e-6], [5e-6, 100.0], [7e-6, 8e-6]], 1024.0, FP16)
=> (False, None, 512.0)
--- check test | On a skip, only the one-number flags are communicated
(lambda c: (scaled_dp_step(c, [[1e-6, 2e-6], [3e-6, 4e-6], [5e-6, 100.0], [7e-6, 8e-6]], 1024.0, FP16), len(c.log) == 2 * 4 * 3 and all(n in (0, 4) for s, d, n in c.log))[1])(make_cluster(1, 4))
--- check test | A good step communicates the flags and then the gradients
(lambda c: (scaled_dp_step(c, [[1e-6, 2e-6], [3e-6, 4e-6], [5e-6, 6e-6], [7e-6, 8e-6]], 1024.0, FP16), len(c.log) == 2 * 2 * 4 * 3)[1])(make_cluster(1, 4))
--- check test | Without a scale tiny gradients underflow; with one they survive
(lambda a, b: a[1] == [0.0] and abs(b[1][0] - 2e-9) < 1e-12)(scaled_dp_step(make_cluster(1, 2), [[1e-9], [3e-9]], 1.0, FP16), scaled_dp_step(make_cluster(1, 2), [[1e-9], [3e-9]], 65536.0, FP16))

+++ problem | ZeRO-3 forward pass with prefetching
--- task
In ZeRO stage 3 each layer's weights must be all-gathered before the layer can run. One layer's all-gather over `n_ranks` ranks takes (n_ranks − 1) · (alpha + 2·n / (n_ranks · bw)) seconds for a layer of n parameters in bf16 (and 0 on one rank); its compute takes `layer_compute[i]` seconds.

Write `zero3_forward_time(layer_params, layer_compute, n_ranks, alpha, bw, prefetch)`: the time of one forward pass through the layers in order.

- Without prefetching, gather layer i, then compute it, then gather layer i + 1, and so on.
- With prefetching, gathers run on their own channel, one at a time, while compute runs on the device. Layer i's gather starts when layer i − 1's gather has finished **and** layer i − 1's compute has started (so at most two layers' full weights are held at once); layer 0's starts at 0. Layer i's compute starts when its gather has finished and layer i − 1's compute has finished.

Return the time when the last compute finishes (0.0 for no layers).
--- starter
def zero3_forward_time(layer_params, layer_compute, n_ranks, alpha, bw, prefetch):
    return sum(layer_compute)
--- solution
def zero3_forward_time(layer_params, layer_compute, n_ranks, alpha, bw, prefetch):
    gathers = [(n_ranks - 1) * (alpha + 2 * n / (n_ranks * bw)) if n_ranks > 1 else 0.0 for n in layer_params]
    if not prefetch:
        return sum(gathers) + sum(layer_compute)
    gather_end, compute_start, compute_end = [], [], []
    for i, (g, c) in enumerate(zip(gathers, layer_compute)):
        begin = 0.0 if i == 0 else max(gather_end[i - 1], compute_start[i - 1])
        gather_end.append(begin + g)
        start = max(gather_end[i], compute_end[i - 1] if i else 0.0)
        compute_start.append(start)
        compute_end.append(start + c)
    return compute_end[-1] if compute_end else 0.0
--- check test | Four 7B-sized layers on 32 ranks, without and with prefetching
abs(zero3_forward_time([12 * 4096 * 4096] * 4, [0.01] * 4, 32, 10e-6, 50e9, False) - 0.07244562176) < 1e-12 and abs(zero3_forward_time([12 * 4096 * 4096] * 4, [0.01] * 4, 32, 10e-6, 50e9, True) - 0.04811140544) < 1e-12
--- check test | Long compute hides every gather but the first
abs(zero3_forward_time([12 * 4096 * 4096] * 4, [0.05] * 4, 32, 10e-6, 50e9, True) - 0.20811140544) < 1e-12
--- check case | Gathers paced by compute starts
zero3_forward_time([1e9, 1e9, 1e9], [0.5, 0.1, 0.5], 2, 0.0, 2e9, True)
=> 2.0
--- check case | One rank needs no gathers; no layers take no time
(zero3_forward_time([100, 100], [1.0, 1.0], 1, 1e-6, 1e9, True), zero3_forward_time([], [], 8, 1e-6, 1e9, True))
=> (2.0, 0.0)

+++ problem | A pipeline with uneven stages
--- task
Generalize lesson 06's pipeline simulator to stages of different speeds. `f[s]` and `b[s]` are stage s's forward and backward times for one micro-batch (the number of stages is `len(f)`). The schedules and dependency rules are lesson 06's: `"gpipe"` runs all forwards then all backwards on every stage; `"1f1b"` runs min(p − s − 1, m) warm-up forwards, then forward-backward pairs, then the remaining backwards. A forward on stage s > 0 waits for the same micro-batch's forward on stage s − 1; a backward on stage s < p − 1 waits for the same micro-batch's backward on stage s + 1, and on the last stage for its own forward.

Write `pipeline_uneven(f, b, m, schedule)` returning the time the last task ends.
--- starter
def pipeline_uneven(f, b, m, schedule):
    return m * max(x + y for x, y in zip(f, b))
--- solution
def stage_order(p, m, s, schedule):
    forwards = [("F", j) for j in range(m)]
    backwards = [("B", j) for j in range(m)]
    if schedule == "gpipe":
        return forwards + backwards
    w = min(p - s - 1, m)
    order = forwards[:w]
    for i in range(m - w):
        order += [forwards[w + i], backwards[i]]
    return order + backwards[m - w:]


def pipeline_uneven(f, b, m, schedule):
    p = len(f)
    orders = [stage_order(p, m, s, schedule) for s in range(p)]
    finished = {}
    free = [0.0] * p
    nxt = [0] * p
    left = 2 * m * p
    while left:
        for s in range(p):
            while nxt[s] < len(orders[s]):
                kind, j = orders[s][nxt[s]]
                if kind == "F":
                    dep = ("F", j, s - 1) if s > 0 else None
                else:
                    dep = ("B", j, s + 1) if s < p - 1 else ("F", j, s)
                if dep is not None and dep not in finished:
                    break
                free[s] = max(free[s], finished[dep] if dep else 0.0) + (f[s] if kind == "F" else b[s])
                finished[(kind, j, s)] = free[s]
                nxt[s] += 1
                left -= 1
    return max(free)
--- check case | Equal stages give lesson 06's (m + p - 1)(f + b)
(pipeline_uneven([1, 1, 1, 1], [2, 2, 2, 2], 8, "gpipe"), pipeline_uneven([1, 1, 1, 1], [2, 2, 2, 2], 8, "1f1b"))
=> (33.0, 33.0)
--- check case | One slow stage
(pipeline_uneven([1, 2, 1, 1], [2, 4, 2, 2], 8, "gpipe"), pipeline_uneven([1, 2, 1, 1], [2, 4, 2, 2], 8, "1f1b"))
=> (57.0, 53.0)
--- check case | Stages slow in different directions
(pipeline_uneven([1, 1.5, 1], [2, 2, 3], 5, "gpipe"), pipeline_uneven([1, 1.5, 1], [2, 2, 3], 5, "1f1b"))
=> (28.5, 26.5)
--- check case | One stage
pipeline_uneven([3], [5], 4, "1f1b")
=> 32.0

+++ problem | The smallest cluster that meets a deadline
--- task
A team must train a model on `total_tokens` tokens within `days` days and wants to rent as few nodes (of 8 A-1 devices) as possible. Write `smallest_nodes(model, node_options, seq, global_seqs, total_tokens, days)`: try the node counts in `node_options` from smallest to largest; for each, plan with lesson 07's `best_plan` on `make_cluster(nodes, 8)`; if a plan fits, the run takes ⌈total_tokens / (global_seqs · seq)⌉ steps of `plan_step_time` seconds. Return `(nodes, plan, days_needed)` for the first node count that finishes within `days`, with days_needed rounded to 2 places, or `None` if none does. The starter has the whole planner.
--- starter
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


class Model:
    """The shape of a GPT-style transformer (lesson 03)."""

    def __init__(self, name, layers, hidden, heads, vocab):
        self.name = name
        self.layers = layers
        self.hidden = hidden
        self.heads = heads
        self.vocab = vocab

    def params(self):
        return 12 * self.layers * self.hidden ** 2 + self.vocab * self.hidden


def state_bytes(model):
    return 16 * model.params()


def layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch):
    return seq * micro_batch * (34 * model.hidden + 5 * model.heads * seq)


def activation_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    if recompute == "none":
        return model.layers * layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return model.layers * 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return model.layers * 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden + layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


class Cluster:
    """Devices in nodes, joined by links (lesson 04). Ranks 0 .. size()-1, node by node."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, per_node, accel, intra_bw, intra_lat, inter_bw, inter_lat):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.per_node = per_node
        self.accel = accel
        self.intra_bw = intra_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, between two devices in one node
        self.intra_lat = intra_lat    # seconds of latency per message inside a node
        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes
        self.inter_lat = inter_lat    # seconds of latency per message between nodes
        self.log = []                 # every message sent: (src, dst, nbytes)

    def size(self):
        return self.nodes * self.per_node

    def node_of(self, rank):
        return rank // self.per_node

    def message_time(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        if self.node_of(src) == self.node_of(dst):
            return self.intra_lat + nbytes / self.intra_bw
        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw

    def send(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        """Send one message: record it and return how long it takes."""
        self.log.append((src, dst, nbytes))
        return self.message_time(src, dst, nbytes)


def make_cluster(nodes, per_node):
    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


MATMUL_EFFICIENCY = 0.7   # assumption: well-tuned matrix multiplies reach about 70% of peak


def ring_time(p, nbytes, alpha, bw):
    """Alpha-beta time of a ring all-reduce of nbytes over p ranks on identical links."""
    if p == 1:
        return 0.0
    return 2 * (p - 1) * (alpha + nbytes / (p * bw))


def per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    """Activation bytes one layer keeps per micro-batch (lesson 03), before tensor parallelism."""
    if recompute == "none":
        return layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


def candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
    """Every plan (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute) that uses the whole cluster legally."""
    devices = cluster.size()
    for tp in range(1, cluster.per_node + 1):
        if cluster.per_node % tp or model.heads % tp:
            continue
        for pp in range(1, model.layers + 1):
            if model.layers % pp or devices % (tp * pp):
                continue
            dp = devices // (tp * pp)
            if global_seqs % dp:
                continue
            per_rank = global_seqs // dp
            for micro_batch in range(1, per_rank + 1):
                if per_rank % micro_batch:
                    continue
                for recompute in ("none", "selective", "full"):
                    yield (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute)


def plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    slice_params = model.params() / (tp * pp)
    state = 4 * slice_params + 12 * slice_params / dp          # ZeRO stage 1 over dp
    in_flight = min(pp, m)                                     # 1F1B: stage 0 holds this many
    act = in_flight * (model.layers // pp) * per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute) / tp
    if recompute == "full":
        act += layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch) / tp       # the one layer being rebuilt
    return state + act


def plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    devices = dp * tp * pp
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    flops = (8 if recompute == "full" else 6) * model.params() * seq * micro_batch / (tp * pp)
    compute = flops / (cluster.accel.peak_flops * MATMUL_EFFICIENCY)
    act = seq * micro_batch * model.hidden * 2
    tp_comm = (model.layers // pp) * 4 * ring_time(tp, act, cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    if pp == 1:
        p2p = 0.0
    elif tp * pp <= cluster.per_node:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.intra_lat + act / cluster.intra_bw)
    else:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.inter_lat + act / cluster.inter_bw)
    per_micro = compute + tp_comm + p2p
    if devices > cluster.per_node:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.inter_lat, cluster.inter_bw)
    else:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    return (m + pp - 1) * per_micro + dp_comm


def mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    step = plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
    useful = 6 * model.params() * seq * global_seqs
    return useful / (step * dp * tp * pp * cluster.accel.peak_flops)


def best_plan(model, cluster, seq, global_seqs):
    best = None
    for plan in candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
        if plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs) > cluster.accel.mem_bytes:
            continue
        u = mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
        if best is None or u > best[1]:
            best = (plan, u)
    if best is None:
        return None
    return best[0], round(best[1], 4)


def smallest_nodes(model, node_options, seq, global_seqs, total_tokens, days):
    return None
--- solution
class Accelerator:
    """A cost model of one accelerator (lesson 01)."""

    def __init__(self, name, peak_flops, mem_bw, mem_bytes):
        self.name = name
        self.peak_flops = peak_flops   # FLOP per second, dense, in the training format
        self.mem_bw = mem_bw           # bytes per second between memory and compute
        self.mem_bytes = mem_bytes     # bytes of on-device memory

    def ridge(self):
        return self.peak_flops / self.mem_bw

    def attainable(self, intensity):
        return min(self.peak_flops, intensity * self.mem_bw)

    def time(self, flops, nbytes):
        return max(flops / self.peak_flops, nbytes / self.mem_bw)

    def bound(self, flops, nbytes):
        if flops / self.peak_flops >= nbytes / self.mem_bw:
            return "compute"
        return "memory"


def matmul_flops(m, k, n):
    return 2 * m * k * n


def matmul_bytes(m, k, n, bytes_per):
    return bytes_per * (m * k + k * n + m * n)


ACCEL = Accelerator("A-1", 1.0e15, 3.0e12, 80e9)


class Model:
    """The shape of a GPT-style transformer (lesson 03)."""

    def __init__(self, name, layers, hidden, heads, vocab):
        self.name = name
        self.layers = layers
        self.hidden = hidden
        self.heads = heads
        self.vocab = vocab

    def params(self):
        return 12 * self.layers * self.hidden ** 2 + self.vocab * self.hidden


def state_bytes(model):
    return 16 * model.params()


def layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch):
    return seq * micro_batch * (34 * model.hidden + 5 * model.heads * seq)


def activation_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    if recompute == "none":
        return model.layers * layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return model.layers * 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return model.layers * 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden + layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


class Cluster:
    """Devices in nodes, joined by links (lesson 04). Ranks 0 .. size()-1, node by node."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, per_node, accel, intra_bw, intra_lat, inter_bw, inter_lat):
        self.nodes = nodes
        self.per_node = per_node
        self.accel = accel
        self.intra_bw = intra_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, between two devices in one node
        self.intra_lat = intra_lat    # seconds of latency per message inside a node
        self.inter_bw = inter_bw      # bytes/s, one direction, per device, between nodes
        self.inter_lat = inter_lat    # seconds of latency per message between nodes
        self.log = []                 # every message sent: (src, dst, nbytes)

    def size(self):
        return self.nodes * self.per_node

    def node_of(self, rank):
        return rank // self.per_node

    def message_time(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        if self.node_of(src) == self.node_of(dst):
            return self.intra_lat + nbytes / self.intra_bw
        return self.inter_lat + nbytes / self.inter_bw

    def send(self, src, dst, nbytes):
        """Send one message: record it and return how long it takes."""
        self.log.append((src, dst, nbytes))
        return self.message_time(src, dst, nbytes)


def make_cluster(nodes, per_node):
    """The course's cluster: A-1 devices, 300 GB/s and 3 us in a node, 50 GB/s and 10 us between nodes."""
    return Cluster(nodes, per_node, ACCEL, 300e9, 3e-6, 50e9, 10e-6)


MATMUL_EFFICIENCY = 0.7   # assumption: well-tuned matrix multiplies reach about 70% of peak


def ring_time(p, nbytes, alpha, bw):
    """Alpha-beta time of a ring all-reduce of nbytes over p ranks on identical links."""
    if p == 1:
        return 0.0
    return 2 * (p - 1) * (alpha + nbytes / (p * bw))


def per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute):
    """Activation bytes one layer keeps per micro-batch (lesson 03), before tensor parallelism."""
    if recompute == "none":
        return layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch)
    if recompute == "selective":
        return 34 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    if recompute == "full":
        return 2 * seq * micro_batch * model.hidden
    raise ValueError(f"unknown recompute: {recompute}")


def candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
    """Every plan (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute) that uses the whole cluster legally."""
    devices = cluster.size()
    for tp in range(1, cluster.per_node + 1):
        if cluster.per_node % tp or model.heads % tp:
            continue
        for pp in range(1, model.layers + 1):
            if model.layers % pp or devices % (tp * pp):
                continue
            dp = devices // (tp * pp)
            if global_seqs % dp:
                continue
            per_rank = global_seqs // dp
            for micro_batch in range(1, per_rank + 1):
                if per_rank % micro_batch:
                    continue
                for recompute in ("none", "selective", "full"):
                    yield (dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute)


def plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    slice_params = model.params() / (tp * pp)
    state = 4 * slice_params + 12 * slice_params / dp          # ZeRO stage 1 over dp
    in_flight = min(pp, m)                                     # 1F1B: stage 0 holds this many
    act = in_flight * (model.layers // pp) * per_layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch, recompute) / tp
    if recompute == "full":
        act += layer_bytes(model, seq, micro_batch) / tp       # the one layer being rebuilt
    return state + act


def plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    devices = dp * tp * pp
    m = global_seqs // (dp * micro_batch)
    flops = (8 if recompute == "full" else 6) * model.params() * seq * micro_batch / (tp * pp)
    compute = flops / (cluster.accel.peak_flops * MATMUL_EFFICIENCY)
    act = seq * micro_batch * model.hidden * 2
    tp_comm = (model.layers // pp) * 4 * ring_time(tp, act, cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    if pp == 1:
        p2p = 0.0
    elif tp * pp <= cluster.per_node:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.intra_lat + act / cluster.intra_bw)
    else:
        p2p = 2 * (cluster.inter_lat + act / cluster.inter_bw)
    per_micro = compute + tp_comm + p2p
    if devices > cluster.per_node:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.inter_lat, cluster.inter_bw)
    else:
        dp_comm = ring_time(dp, 2 * model.params() / (tp * pp), cluster.intra_lat, cluster.intra_bw)
    return (m + pp - 1) * per_micro + dp_comm


def mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster):
    dp, tp, pp, micro_batch, recompute = plan
    step = plan_step_time(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
    useful = 6 * model.params() * seq * global_seqs
    return useful / (step * dp * tp * pp * cluster.accel.peak_flops)


def best_plan(model, cluster, seq, global_seqs):
    best = None
    for plan in candidates(model, cluster, global_seqs):
        if plan_memory(model, plan, seq, global_seqs) > cluster.accel.mem_bytes:
            continue
        u = mfu(model, plan, seq, global_seqs, cluster)
        if best is None or u > best[1]:
            best = (plan, u)
    if best is None:
        return None
    return best[0], round(best[1], 4)


def smallest_nodes(model, node_options, seq, global_seqs, total_tokens, days):
    steps = -(-total_tokens // (global_seqs * seq))
    for nodes in sorted(node_options):
        cluster = make_cluster(nodes, 8)
        found = best_plan(model, cluster, seq, global_seqs)
        if found is None:
            continue
        needed = steps * plan_step_time(model, found[0], seq, global_seqs, cluster) / 86400
        if needed <= days:
            return nodes, found[0], round(needed, 2)
    return None
--- check case | 13B on 260 billion tokens within 4 days
smallest_nodes(Model("13B", 40, 5120, 40, 50304), [4, 8, 16, 32], 2048, 1024, 260 * 10 ** 9, 4)
=> (16, (16, 1, 8, 1, "none"), 3.06)
--- check case | Options in any order; a looser deadline
smallest_nodes(Model("13B", 40, 5120, 40, 50304), [32, 4, 16, 8], 2048, 1024, 260 * 10 ** 9, 10)
=> (8, (8, 1, 8, 1, "none"), 5.65)
--- check case | An impossible deadline
smallest_nodes(Model("13B", 40, 5120, 40, 50304), [4, 8], 2048, 1024, 260 * 10 ** 9, 1)
=> None
--- check case | 70B skips the cluster sizes it does not fit on
smallest_nodes(Model("70B", 80, 8192, 64, 50304), [1, 2, 4, 8, 16], 2048, 1024, 10 ** 11, 30)
=> (4, (4, 2, 4, 1, "selective"), 21.99)

+++ problem | How deep a prefetch queue
--- task
Write `min_depth(load_times, step_time, budget, max_depth)`: the smallest prefetch depth d from 0 to `max_depth` for which lesson 08's `simulate_loader(load_times, step_time, d)` (in the starter) stalls for at most `budget` seconds in total, or `None` if no depth up to `max_depth` does.
--- starter
def simulate_loader(load_times, step_time, depth):
    starts, ends = [], []
    loader_free = 0.0
    stall = 0.0
    for i, load in enumerate(load_times):
        if depth == 0:
            allowed = ends[i - 1] if i > 0 else 0.0
        else:
            allowed = starts[i - depth] if i >= depth else 0.0
        loader_free = max(loader_free, allowed) + load
        previous_end = ends[i - 1] if i > 0 else 0.0
        start = max(loader_free, previous_end)
        stall += start - previous_end
        starts.append(start)
        ends.append(start + step_time)
    total = ends[-1] if ends else 0.0
    return round(total, 6), round(stall, 6)


def min_depth(load_times, step_time, budget, max_depth):
    return max_depth
--- solution
def simulate_loader(load_times, step_time, depth):
    starts, ends = [], []
    loader_free = 0.0
    stall = 0.0
    for i, load in enumerate(load_times):
        if depth == 0:
            allowed = ends[i - 1] if i > 0 else 0.0
        else:
            allowed = starts[i - depth] if i >= depth else 0.0
        loader_free = max(loader_free, allowed) + load
        previous_end = ends[i - 1] if i > 0 else 0.0
        start = max(loader_free, previous_end)
        stall += start - previous_end
        starts.append(start)
        ends.append(start + step_time)
    total = ends[-1] if ends else 0.0
    return round(total, 6), round(stall, 6)


def min_depth(load_times, step_time, budget, max_depth):
    for d in range(max_depth + 1):
        if simulate_loader(load_times, step_time, d)[1] <= budget:
            return d
    return None
--- check case | A slow load late in the run
(min_depth([0.5] * 5 + [3.0] + [0.5] * 4, 1.0, 0.5, 10), min_depth([0.5] * 5 + [3.0] + [0.5] * 4, 1.0, 1.5, 10))
=> (3, 2)
--- check case | A bigger spike needs a deeper queue
min_depth([0.5] * 10 + [4.0] + [0.5] * 5, 1.0, 0.5, 10)
=> 4
--- check case | Two slow loads in a row, with a looser budget
(min_depth([0.6] * 8 + [2.5, 2.5] + [0.6] * 6, 1.0, 0.5, 10), min_depth([0.6] * 8 + [2.5, 2.5] + [0.6] * 6, 1.0, 1.0, 10))
=> (None, 4)
--- check case | Loads slower than steps: no depth helps
min_depth([2.0] * 5, 1.0, 1.0, 8)
=> None
--- check case | No batches, and a budget only depth 0 is allowed to try
(min_depth([], 1.0, 0.0, 3), min_depth([0.2] * 3, 1.0, 0.2, 0))
=> (0, None)

+++ problem | Asynchronous checkpoints in the failure simulator
--- task
Extend lesson 09's failure simulator to asynchronous checkpoints. All times are in hours of wall clock. The run needs `work` hours of computation. After every `interval` hours of work since the last snapshot, training pauses for `block` hours to copy a **snapshot** (no progress during the pause; nothing is taken after the work is finished). The snapshot is then written in the background for `write` hours while training continues, and becomes **durable** `write` hours after its pause ends. Several writes may be in flight.

At a failure (`failures` is a sorted list of times), the job restores the durable snapshot with the most work, among those that became durable at or before the failure time (0 work if none), discards every snapshot not yet durable, and resumes `restart` hours after the failure (a failure during a restart starts the restart over from that failure). A failure during a pause loses that snapshot. The count of work since the last snapshot restarts at 0 after a restore.

Write `simulate_async(work, interval, block, write, restart, failures)` returning `(wall_time, failures_hit)` like `simulate_run`, with the time rounded to 6 places.
--- starter
import math


def simulate_async(work, interval, block, write, restart, failures):
    snapshots = math.ceil(work / interval) - 1
    return round(work + snapshots * block, 6), 0
--- solution
import math


def simulate_async(work, interval, block, write, restart, failures):
    t = 0.0
    done = 0.0            # work in the current state of the model
    since = 0.0           # work since the last snapshot
    restore_point = 0.0   # work in the best durable snapshot
    pending = []          # (durable_at, work) of snapshots being written
    hit = 0

    def next_failure():
        return failures[hit] if hit < len(failures) else math.inf

    def fail():
        nonlocal t, hit, done, since, pending, restore_point
        f = next_failure()
        for at, w in pending:
            if at <= f:
                restore_point = max(restore_point, w)
        pending = []
        t = f + restart
        hit += 1
        while next_failure() < t:
            t = next_failure() + restart
            hit += 1
        done, since = restore_point, 0.0

    while True:
        for at, w in pending:
            if at <= t:
                restore_point = max(restore_point, w)
        pending = [(at, w) for at, w in pending if at > t]
        segment = min(interval - since, work - done)
        if next_failure() < t + segment:
            fail()
            continue
        t += segment
        done += segment
        since += segment
        if done >= work:
            return round(t, 6), hit
        if next_failure() < t + block:
            fail()
            continue
        t += block
        since = 0.0
        pending.append((t + write, done))
--- check case | No failures: work plus the pauses
simulate_async(10, 3, 0.1, 1.0, 1, [])
=> (10.3, 0)
--- check case | A failure before the first snapshot is durable loses everything
simulate_async(10, 3, 0.1, 1.0, 1, [3.5])
=> (14.8, 1)
--- check case | A failure after it is durable keeps 3 hours of work
simulate_async(10, 3, 0.1, 1.0, 1, [4.5])
=> (12.7, 1)
--- check case | A failure during a pause
simulate_async(10, 3, 0.1, 1.0, 1, [3.05])
=> (14.35, 1)
--- check case | Slow writes: the older durable snapshot is the one restored
simulate_async(10, 3, 0.1, 5.0, 1, [8.5, 8.7])
=> (16.9, 2)
--- check case | Instant writes behave like synchronous checkpoints
simulate_async(10, 3, 0.1, 0.0, 1, [4.0])
=> (12.2, 1)

+++ question | Arithmetic intensity of an update
--- ask
The kernel y = a·x + y runs over n fp32 numbers (a is one number held in a register). It reads x and y and writes y. What is its arithmetic intensity, in FLOPs per byte? Type a fraction or a decimal.
--- answer
1/6
0.167
0.1667
0.16
0.17
--- why
Each element costs one multiply and one add, 2 FLOPs, and moves 12 bytes: 4 to read x, 4 to read y, 4 to write y. 2 / 12 = 1/6, far left of A-1's ridge of about 333, so the kernel is completely memory-bound.

+++ question | Rounding near 4,096 in fp16
--- ask
fp16 has 10 mantissa bits. What does `round_to(4097.0, FP16)` return? Type the number.
--- answer
4096
4096.0
--- why
Between 4,096 = 2¹² and 8,192, fp16 numbers are 2^(12 − 10) = 4 apart. 4,097 is 1 above 4,096 and 3 below 4,100, so it rounds to 4,096. (A running sum in fp16 that reaches 4,096 can no longer grow by 1 at all.)

+++ question | Sixteen bytes per parameter
--- ask
Where do the 16 bytes per parameter of mixed-precision training with Adam come from?
--- choice
16-bit weights and gradients, 2 bytes each, plus 12 bytes of activations per parameter.
--- choice correct
bf16 weights (2) and gradients (2), plus an fp32 master copy (4) and Adam's two fp32 moments (4 + 4).
--- choice
fp32 weights, gradients, and two Adam moments, 4 bytes each.
--- why
The forward and backward passes use bf16 weights and produce bf16 gradients; the optimizer keeps fp32 master weights so small updates are not rounded away, and Adam's m and v in fp32. Activations are separate and depend on the batch, not on the parameter count.

+++ question | What each rank sends in a ring all-reduce
--- ask
A ring all-reduce averages a 1 GB gradient over 4 ranks. How many gigabytes does each rank send in total? Type the number.
--- answer
1.5
1.5 GB
--- why
Each rank sends 2(p − 1) messages of n/p bytes: 6 messages of 0.25 GB, so 2(p − 1)/p · n = 1.5 GB. As p grows this approaches 2 GB, never more, which is why the ring is bandwidth-optimal.

+++ question | ZeRO stage 2 in numbers
--- ask
A model of 10 × 10⁹ parameters is trained with ZeRO stage 2 on 10 devices. How many GB (10⁹ bytes) of model state does each device hold? Type the number.
--- answer
34
34 GB
--- why
Stage 2 keeps the full bf16 weights (2Ψ) and shards gradients and optimizer state (14Ψ/N): 2 × 10 + 14 × 10 / 10 = 20 + 14 = 34 GB.

+++ question | Spot the bug in a tensor-parallel MLP
--- ask
Each of t devices holds column group A_i of A and row group B_i of B. Which version of the device's code computes its share of Y = GELU(X·A)·B correctly, before the all-reduce adds the shares?
--- choice correct
`partial = matmul([[gelu(v) for v in row] for row in matmul(X, A_i)], B_i)`
--- choice
`partial = [[gelu(v) for v in row] for row in matmul(matmul(X, A_i), B_i)]`
--- choice
`partial = matmul(matmul(X, A_i), B_i)`, with GELU applied after the all-reduce.
--- why
GELU must act on the hidden layer, X·A, which a column-parallel device holds completely for its own columns. Applying it after B (or after the all-reduce) applies a nonlinearity to the wrong thing: GELU(X·A)·B is not GELU(X·A·B), and GELU of a sum of partial results is not the sum of their GELUs.

+++ question | The bubble of a pipeline
--- ask
A pipeline has 8 stages and runs 24 micro-batches per step. What fraction of each device's time is bubble? Type a fraction or a decimal.
--- answer
7/31
0.226
0.2258
0.23
--- why
The bubble fraction is (p − 1)/(m + p − 1) = 7/(24 + 7) = 7/31 ≈ 0.226, for GPipe and 1F1B alike. Doubling the micro-batches to 48 would bring it down to 7/55.

+++ question | Reading an MFU
--- ask
A 10-billion-parameter model trains at 1 million tokens per second on 128 accelerators of 10¹⁵ FLOP/s each. About what is its MFU?
--- choice
About 8%, because 10¹⁰ × 10⁶ is far below 1.28 × 10¹⁷.
--- choice correct
About 47%: 6 × 10¹⁰ × 10⁶ = 6 × 10¹⁶ useful FLOP/s out of 1.28 × 10¹⁷.
--- choice
About 63%, counting 8 FLOPs per parameter per token for recomputation.
--- why
Training costs 6 FLOPs per parameter per token (2 forward, 4 backward), so the useful rate is 6 × 10¹⁶ FLOP/s, and 6 × 10¹⁶ / 1.28 × 10¹⁷ ≈ 0.47. MFU never counts recomputed FLOPs.

+++ question | The replica with one fewer batch
--- ask
Shards are split over 8 data-parallel replicas, and one replica gets one shard fewer than the others. What happens at the end of the epoch?
--- choice
That replica idles for a step while the others finish, which wastes a little time.
--- choice correct
The others block forever in the gradient all-reduce that the finished replica never joins: the job hangs.
--- choice
The all-reduce averages over 7 replicas for the last step instead of 8.
--- why
A collective needs every rank to call it. The replica that ran out of data stops calling the all-reduce, so the other seven wait for its messages indefinitely. That is why shard assignment gives every replica exactly the same count and drops the leftovers.

+++ question | A checkpoint interval
--- ask
A job runs on 8,000 devices, each with an MTBF of 40,000 hours, and a checkpoint stops training for 0.05 hours. About how often should it checkpoint, by Young's formula?
--- choice
About every 63 hours, √(2 × 0.05 × 40,000).
--- choice correct
About every 42 minutes: the cluster MTBF is 40,000 / 8,000 = 5 hours, and √(2 × 0.05 × 5) ≈ 0.71 hours.
--- choice
About every 5 hours, once per expected failure.
--- why
The job fails whenever any device does, so its MTBF is M / N = 5 hours. Young's interval is √(2·δ·M_c) = √0.5 ≈ 0.707 hours. Using one device's MTBF gives an interval √8,000 ≈ 89 times too long.
